### 1. Nvidia and AI-chip sell-off

**Question**: Is the Nvidia and AI-chip sell-off over, or is there more downside after the Southeast Asia shipment halt?

**Why this rank**: NVDA is still #1 on both Reddit boards with about 23,000 upvotes, far ahead of everything else, even though mentions are down about 70-75% in the Thanksgiving lull.

**Heat evidence**:
- Ape Wisdom WSB: NVDA mentions / change / upvotes = 124 / -76% / 22,819 (#1)
- Ape Wisdom all stocks: NVDA mentions / change / upvotes = 159 / -71% / 22,981 (#1)
- Ape Wisdom WSB: AMD mentions / change = 39 / -57% (#4)
- Stocktwits trending: SOXL = #2 on the trending list (undated snapshot)

**Drivers**:
- Nvidia fell 3.9% on Nov 25 as AI shares lost steam for a second day. ([source](https://eurasiabusinessnews.com/2026/11/27/stock-markets-today-november-26-2026-european-and-asian-stocks-rally-as-tariff-fears-ease/))
- FT reported Nvidia told partners to halt AI server shipments to Malaysia, Singapore, Thailand and Indonesia after new US export-license rules. ([source](https://www.usnews.com/news/top-news/articles/2026-11-26/nvidia-halts-server-shipments-to-four-southeast-asian-countries-ft-reports))
- Investors continue to question the sustainability of AI spending and the debt financing data-center build-outs. ([source](https://247wallst.com/investing/2026/11/25/micron-falls-7-as-a-weaker-dram-outlook-and-new-tariffs-hit-memory-stocks-sandisk-drops-7-are-micron-and-memory-stocks-now-a-value-play/))

### 2. Hot PCE inflation, December Fed hike and new tariffs

**Question**: With inflation sticky and new tariffs proposed, is a December Fed hike locked in, and how many more follow?

**Why this rank**: This is the macro backdrop behind the AI sell-off; SPY is #2 on both Reddit boards and markets are heavily positioned for a 25 bp December hike.

**Heat evidence**:
- Ape Wisdom WSB: SPY mentions / change = 63 / -87% (#2)
- Ape Wisdom all stocks: SPY mentions / change = 87 / -83% (#2)
- Kitco / CNBC: US 10-year yield = About 4.80-4.85%, a 2.5-month low (holiday trading)
- CNBC: Dollar index = About 103.7, flat

**Drivers**:
- The administration proposed tariffs of 20-40% on 14 trading partners including China, Japan and the EU after an overcapacity investigation; steel, aluminum and autos are excluded. ([source](https://wisevoter.com/world/us/2026/11/26/fed-holds-rates-trump-proposes-tariffs-14-partners))
- The fed funds rate is 3.75-4.00% after a September hike; markets still price a further 25 bp increase in December and early 2027, not cuts. ([source](https://www.kitco.com/news/article/2026-11-26/gold-slips-silver-steadies-fed-rate-hike-expectations-persist-holiday))
- Long-end Treasury yields fell as investors bought bonds despite sticky inflation data. ([source](https://www.cnbc.com/amp/2026/11/26/dollar-yields-treasury-safe-heaven-federal-reserve.html))

### 4. Bitcoin near $61,000 in extreme fear

**Question**: Is Bitcoin's bounce from around $60,000 a real bottom or a short squeeze before another leg down?

**Why this rank**: Bitcoin is far ahead of every other coin on Reddit and sits at the top of CoinGecko's trending list, and the Fear & Greed Index is at 12.

**Heat evidence**:
- Ape Wisdom crypto: BTC mentions / 24h ago / upvotes = 104 / 172 / 251 (#1)

**Drivers**:
- A short squeeze drove the rebound: about $139.7M of BTC shorts and $68.5M of ETH shorts were liquidated in 24 hours. ([source](https://www.fool.com/coverage/stock-market-today/2026/11/25/crypto-market-today-nov-25-bitcoin-ethereum-and-solana-as-short-liquidations-fuel-fragile-rebound/))

([source](https://www.tradingview.com/news/benzinga:fbba138e6094b:0-bitcoin-ethereum-xrp-dogecoin-steady-on-thanksgiving-top-analyst-says-btc-rebound-to-100-000-will-reinforce-apex-crypto-as-super-cockroach/))

### 5. Memory chips: Micron and SanDisk slump

**Question**: Are Micron and SanDisk now a value buy, or is the memory cycle rolling over?

**Why this rank**: MU and SNDK rank #5-#8 on the Reddit boards and the story dominated the last two sessions, though mentions fell about 77% in the holiday lull.

- Ape Wisdom WSB: MU mentions / change = 34 / -78% (#5)
- Ape Wisdom WSB: SNDK mentions / change = 26 / -76% (#6)
- Search coverage: MU close Nov 25 = $601.25, -4.5% after an intraday drop of about 13% (sources conflict)

**Drivers**:
- TrendForce cut its outlook on Nov 25: Chinese cloud providers reduced 2027 memory orders and NAND contract prices are seen falling from 1Q27. ([source](https://invezz.com/news/2026/11/25/micron-sandisk-continue-their-bloodbath-how-long-will-the-pain-continue/))
- BNP Paribas Exane downgraded Micron to Hold and UBS cut its price target; new Section 301 tariffs added to the pressure. ### 6. SpaceX and space stocks

**Question**: Does the FCC's approval of 1 million orbital data-center satellites make SpaceX and space stocks a buy, or is it priced in?

**Why this rank**: SPCX is on the Reddit boards (#15 WSB) and was the most-discussed name in Stocktwits Nov 26 posts, with RKLB also on the Stocktwits trending list; this is the clearest non-AI-chip stock story today.

**Heat evidence**:
- Ape Wisdom WSB: SPCX / ASTS mentions = 11 (#15) / 11 (#14), both about -78% to -81%
- Stocktwits trending: RKLB position = #5 (undated snapshot)
- Search coverage: SPCX close Nov 25 = $127.44, +1.3% after falling as much as 9% intraday

**Drivers**:
- The FCC conditionally approved up to 1 million Starmind orbital data-center satellites on Nov 25; Amazon and Viasat opposed. ([source](https://www.cnbc.com/2026/11/25/spacex-fcc-satellite-approval.html))
- Launches are targeted for 2027 with no cost or revenue estimate; shares fell as much as 9% before recovering. ([source](https://finance.yahoo.com/markets/stocks/articles/spcx-stock-rebounds-9-drop-002241653.html))

### 8. A-shares: CPO, 6G and precious metals

### 10. Monero and Zcash slide

**Question**: Why did Monero fall about 10% and are privacy coins being squeezed out of exchanges?

**Why this rank**: XMR is the biggest decliner among the top 20 coins and #6 on CoinGecko trending, but Reddit crypto boards barely mention it.

**Heat evidence**:
- CoinGecko markets: XMR price / 24h = $467.19 / -10.05% (rank #13)
- CoinGecko trending: XMR position / 24h = #6 / -9.5%
- CoinGecko markets: ZEC price / 24h = $758.11 / -1.48%

**Drivers**:
- KuCoin will delist four XMR spot pairs on Nov 30; XMR dropped about 10.5% to about $450. ([source](https://www.cryptotimes.io/2026/11/26/monero-price-drops-10-as-kucoin-confirms-delisting-of-four-xmr-pairs/))
- A bridged-Monero token (XMR1) collapsed on Hyperliquid on Nov 20 after an attacker sold; native XMR fell far less. ([source](https://www.cryptopolitan.com/bridged-xmr1-goes-to-zero-attacker-sells-on-hyperliquid/))

### Watchlist

- HYPE: Hyperliquid is #3 on CoinGecko trending (+6.6% in 24h, market cap rank 10) and #7 on Stocktwits; no confirmed cause today.
- IQ: iQIYI is #12 on WSB with mentions +100% and 906 upvotes; it trades near $0.50 with a Nasdaq minimum-bid issue and no new catalyst found.
- VKTX: Viking Therapeutics is #1 on the Stocktwits trending list (undated snapshot); no cause found.

### Source issues

- US stock markets were closed for Thanksgiving, so Reddit mentions reflect a holiday lull and the Stocktwits trending page has no date.
- Ape Wisdom stock boards show one percentage per row; it is labelled as 24h change here but the page also mentions a 30-day trend.
- Sina's 20:00 report claims ChiNext -4.19%, contradicting the 17:00 and 23:00 reports and 21jingji (ChiNext +0.29%); the close figures are used.
- Oil evidence (rank 9) is a single low-quality aggregator; the Crypto Briefing item calling Trump 'former President' was ignored.
- Ape Wisdom crypto API fields rank_24h_ago/mentions_24h_ago of 0 or null were treated as no prior ranking.

### 1. Nvidia whipsaw and AI chip sell-off

**Question**: Is the Nvidia and AI-chip sell-off over, or does the Southeast Asia shipment halt mean more downside?

**Why this rank**: NVDA is #1 on both Reddit boards with about 21,000 upvotes, far ahead of every other ticker, though mentions are down about 66-71% in the Thanksgiving lull.

**Heat evidence**:
- Ape Wisdom WSB: NVDA mentions / change / upvotes — 158 / -71% / 21,001 (#1)
- Ape Wisdom all stocks: NVDA mentions / change / upvotes — 194 / -66% / 21,110 (#1)
- Ape Wisdom all stocks: AMD mentions / change — 48 / -64% (#4)
- AltIndex via search: NVDA mentions, 24h — 729

**Drivers**:
- FT reported Nvidia told partners to halt AI server shipments to Malaysia, Singapore, Thailand and Indonesia after new US export-license rules. ([source](https://www.usnews.com/news/top-news/articles/2026-11-26/nvidia-halts-server-shipments-to-four-southeast-asian-countries-ft-reports))
- AI-spending doubts remain the main drag on tech; one strategist calls the market 'very bifurcated' between AI and non-AI stocks. ([source](https://www.cnbc.com/2026/11/26/stock-markets-today-.html))
- Sina's Nov 26 hourly report notes the Philadelphia Semiconductor index fell nearly 15% in three days, pressuring A-shares. ([source](https://k.sina.com.cn/article_7857201856_1d45362c001908w3jx.html))

- The administration proposed new tariffs on 14 trading partners (reports differ: 20% to 40%), possibly effective early next year. ([source](https://wisevoter.com/world/us/2026/11/26/fed-holds-rates-trump-proposes-tariffs-14-partners))
- Waller said a December quarter-point hike would need a signal of a January hike, otherwise 50 bp; Williams pushed back on 'dramatic action'. ([source](https://www.investmentnews.com/equities/pce-inflation-gauge-still-running-hot-as-december-rate-hike-looms/269173))

- Investors increasingly worry that tech companies are overspending on AI infrastructure, per market wraps. ([source](https://www.kiplinger.com/investing/stocks/stocks-rally-off-lows-despite-inflation-and-ai-gloom-stock-market-today))

### 3. December Fed hike and new tariffs

**Question**: After hot PCE inflation and new tariff threats, is a December Fed rate hike a done deal and what does it mean for stocks?

**Why this rank**: Macro driver behind this week's sell-off, with 92-97% odds of a hike priced in and new tariffs on 14 economies; no single ticker board captures it.

**Heat evidence**:
- CME FedWatch via news: December hike odds — about 97%
- Polymarket / Kalshi via news: December hike odds — 94% / 92%
- BEA via news: October PCE / core PCE — 3.8% / 3.3%

**Drivers**:
- October PCE rose 3.8% year over year with core PCE at 3.3%, a three-year high; the December 8-9 meeting is seen as a quarter-point hike, with another expected in January. ([source](https://invezz.com/news/2026/11/25/us-pce-rises-3-8-in-october-experts-say-fed-rate-hike-in-december-a-done-deal/))
- President Trump proposed new tariffs on 14 trading partners including China, the EU and Japan. ([source](https://invezz.com/news/2026/11/25/evening-digest-us-inflation-rises-trump-proposes-fresh-tariffs-on-14-economies/))

### Source issues

- US markets are closed for Thanksgiving, so equity data reflects the Nov 25 session.
- Ape Wisdom WSB page labels its percentage as a 30-day trend, not a 24h change; the all-stocks page gives a 24h change.
- Stocktwits sentiment page gave a ticker list (LTC, NASA, LINK, XRP...) without message volumes; used only for the watchlist. Search snippets of Stocktwits were 13-50 days old and not used.
- Alternative.me gave only a value for each day; CoinGecko trending list is mostly tiny coins (PEJAK, ZYLO), excluded.
- Sources disagree on Micron and SanDisk move size (Micron -4% to -13%, SanDisk -5% to -12%); ranges are quoted, not one figure.
- No Nov 26 social-media-specific coverage was found for the A-share precious metals move; cause not confirmed.

- Stocktwits sentiment page gave tickers and percentages only, no sentiment direction.

- Anthropic (ANT) is not on the Reddit mention boards; its rank rests on news coverage.
